Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any transcription module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 935. The projective resolution has length at most 53 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the transcription space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 935 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 53. QED.
